الدراية والعلم (`quranic:root_000473:B001/m01`); ردع يقرع السامع (`quranic:root_001219:B006/m01`); خلاف الصدق (`quranic:root_001290:B001/m01`); نسبة الشيء أو صاحبه إلى الكذب (`quranic:root_001290:B002/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ح ق ق`: 69:1 (حَآقَّةُ), 69:2 (حَآقَّةُ), 69:3 (حَآقَّةُ); `د ر ي`: 69:3 (أَدْرَىٰ); `ق ر ع`: 69:4 (قَارِعَةِ); `ك ذ ب`: 69:4 (كَذَّبَتْ)","synthesis":"The fixed reality opposes falsehood, then becomes something binding, owed, privately held, contested, defended, and proved. Knowledge supplies the cognitive act, while rebuke can turn the hearer back toward the right and denial supplies the adversarial speech that makes demonstration necessary.","ayah_refs":["69:1","69:2","69:3","69:4"]},{"parent_channel":"P1: Defiance meets coercive mastery","parent_semantic_invariant":"Exceeded limits, refused obedience, and imposed service are answered by a force that masters agents and elements.","parent_surprising_reach":"Transgression shifts between moral rebellion, physical overflow, tyrannical rule, forced labor, and the smooth elevated rock on which resistance finds no purchase.","key":"A","name":"Limit-breaking rebellion and tyrannical command","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"Agents leave obedience, harden into domination, and organize others around a head of error.","active_motifs":"مجاوزة الحد في العصيان (`quranic:root_000936:B001/m01`); الطاغوت رأس الضلالة والطغيان (`quranic:root_000936:B003/m01`); الطاغية المتجبر (`quranic:root_000936:B004/m01`); مجاوزة الحد في العصيان (`quranic:root_000937:B001/m01`); الطاغوت رأس الضلالة (`quranic:root_000937:B003/m01`); العُتُوّ عن الطاعة (`quranic:root_000981:B001/m01`); الخروج عن الطاعة (`quranic:root_001022:B001/m01`); تسخير العامل بلا أجرة (`quranic:root_000685:B002/m01`); عزم ونهوض إلى الأمر (`quranic:root_001273:B003/m01`); رعاية وحفظ وولاية (`quranic:root_001273:B004/m01`); عسر ومنع (`quranic:root_000217:B009/m01`); حمل على أمر (`quranic:root_000217:B010/m01`); كذب عليك بمعنى الزم وعليك به (`quranic:root_001290:B003/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ط غ و`: unavailable (surface root missing); `ط غ ي`: 69:5 (طَّاغِيَةِ), 69:11 (طَغَا); `ع ت و`: 69:6 (عَاتِيَةٍ); `ع ص ي`: 69:10 (عَصَ); `س خ ر`: 69:7 (سَخَّرَ); `ق و م`: 69:7 (قَوْمَ); `ج ب ل`: 69:14 (جِبَالُ); `ك ذ ب`: 69:4 (كَذَّبَتْ)","synthesis":"Moral excess becomes a hierarchy: rebellion crosses a limit, a tyrant or false cultic head directs it, and pride refuses command. Forced labor, obstructive difficulty, compulsion, and an imperative of obligation supply the coercive underside, while resolve and governance show the same capacity turned toward taking charge of an undertaking.","ayah_refs":["69:5","69:11","69:6","69:10","69:7","69:14","69:4"]},{"parent_channel":"P1: Impact becomes capture and defeat","parent_semantic_invariant":"A blow is followed through its full sequence: collision, opposed force, gripping control, collapse, and exhausted aftermath.","parent_surprising_reach":"The sequence reaches from knocking at a door and the clang of iron to wrestling holds, battlefield lines, uprooted bodies, and a road that consumes its traveler.","key":"A","name":"Blow, clang, and battlefield collision","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"Hard surfaces meet, opponents close, and the impact is heard and registered as a battle-event.","active_motifs":"ضرب شيء على شيء (`quranic:root_001219:B001/m01`); مقارعة ومضاربة (`quranic:root_001219:B002/m01`); صرصرة الصوت (`quranic:root_000857:B002/m01`); أثر الضرب وصوت الوقوع (`quranic:root_001675:B004/m01`); وقعة الحرب والإيقاع بالقوم (`quranic:root_001675:B005/m01`); مقاومة ومنازلة (`quranic:root_001273:B014/m01`); أخذة المصارعة (`quranic:root_000018:B011/m01`); صدق الحملة أو كذبها (`quranic:root_001290:B004/m01`); القوة وشدة الوطء (`quranic:root_000482:B006/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ق ر ع`: 69:4 (قَارِعَةِ); `ص ر ص ر`: 69:6 (صَرْصَرٍ); `و ق ع`: 69:15 (وَقَعَتِ/وَاقِعَةُ); `ق و م`: 69:7 (قَوْمَ); `ء خ ذ`: 69:10 (أَخَذَ/أَخْذَةً); `ك ذ ب`: 69:4 (كَذَّبَتْ); `د ك ك`: 69:14 (دُكَّ/دَكَّةً)","synthesis":"Knocking, heroic encounter, the report of a hard fall, and the shock of battle form one collision scene. Wrestling grips and mutual resistance convert sound into control, while the truth or failure of a charge is tested by whether it carries through to thrust and strike.","ayah_refs":["69:4","69:6","69:15","69:7","69:10","69:14"]},{"parent_channel":"P1: Impact becomes capture and defeat","parent_semantic_invariant":"A blow is followed through its full sequence: collision, opposed force, gripping control, collapse, and exhausted aftermath.","parent_surprising_reach":"The sequence reaches from knocking at a door and the clang of iron to wrestling holds, battlefield lines, uprooted bodies, and a road that consumes its traveler.","key":"B","name":"Capture, handle, and arresting grip","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"A target is taken, held by an accessible grip, and prevented from escaping.","active_motifs":"حوز الشيء وتناوله (`quranic:root_000018:B001/m01`); القبض والأسر (`quranic:root_000018:B003/m01`); رقية تمسك وتحبس (`quranic:root_000018:B004/m01`); مقبض الشيء المأخوذ به (`quranic:root_000018:B012/m01`); المدرى والحد المحدد (`quranic:root_000473:B004/m01`); قراع صلب ممسوح (`quranic:root_001219:B011/m01`); آلة قائمة وجزء قائم (`quranic:root_001273:B012/m01`); عود من خشب وطيب وآلة (`quranic:root_001058:B010/m01`); طاقة على المقابلة (`quranic:root_001198:B013/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ء خ ذ`: 69:10 (أَخَذَ/أَخْذَةً); `د ر ي`: 69:3 (أَدْرَىٰ); `ق ر ع`: 69:4 (قَارِعَةِ); `ق و م`: 69:7 (قَوْمَ); `ع و د`: 69:4 (عَادٌۢ), 69:6 (عَادٌ); `ق ب ل`: 69:9 (قَبْلَ)","synthesis":"Taking becomes technically concrete through arrest, a charm that holds, a handle, a pointed implement, a hard stripped surface, a standing machine-part, and a wooden striking tool. The target's lack of capacity to face the force closes the mechanism: there is a grip and no adequate counter-grip.","ayah_refs":["69:10","69:3","69:4","69:7","69:6","69:9"]},{"parent_channel":"P1: Air carries life, cold, and summons","parent_semantic_invariant":"Moving air can animate, overpower, cool, voice, inflate, or leave a body at the moment of death.","parent_surprising_reach":"One medium joins meteorology and physiology: gale, scent, lung, panting, hiccup, roar, and the final departing breath.","key":"D","name":"Relief, alternation, and the return of motion","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"After strain, breath and activity return by rest, alternating labor, or an evening homecoming.","active_motifs":"الرواح والعشي ورد الماشية (`quranic:root_000609:B005/m01`); الراحة والاستراحة والإراحة (`quranic:root_000609:B007/m01`); المراوحة والتناوب (`quranic:root_000609:B008/m01`); السعة والانبساط الحسي (`quranic:root_000609:B009/m01`); الأريحية والنشاط للمعروف (`quranic:root_000609:B010/m01`); أوبة الوعي والقوة والراحة (`quranic:root_001188:B005/m01`); رجوع اللبن بين الحلبتين (`quranic:root_001188:B006/m01`); أخذ الشيء جرعات أو دفعات (`quranic:root_001188:B007/m01`); رجوع بعد انصراف وتثنية بعد بدء (`quranic:root_001058:B001/m01`); عادة ودرَبة ومواظبة (`quranic:root_001058:B004/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ر و ح`: 69:6 (رِيحٍ); `ف و ق`: 69:17 (فَوْقَ); `ع و د`: 69:4 (عَادٌۢ), 69:6 (عَادٌ)","synthesis":"Evening return, rest, alternating work, bodily expansion, and renewed generosity describe recovery as regained room to act. Awakening after illness and the returning milk between milkings give the same rhythm a physiological and pastoral form, while recurrence becomes habit through repeated performance.","ayah_refs":["69:6","69:17","69:4"]},{"parent_channel":"P1: Vertical forms are hollowed, split, and leveled","parent_semantic_invariant":"Tall bodies, palms, buildings, mountains, and sky lose their interior support and vertical distinction.","parent_surprising_reach":"Ruin is rendered through carpentry and anatomy: empty belly, uprooted trunk, loosened wall, torn skin, buried well, and sand ridge all undergo related structural failure.","key":"D","name":"Barren vacancy after failed rain","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"Rain misses or withdraws, growth fails, and place becomes empty, exposed, and lethal to a traveler.","active_motifs":"أرض مأفوكة صرف عنها المطر (`quranic:root_000041:B005/m01`); أرض أخطأها المطر (`quranic:root_000420:B003/m01`); أرض هلكين لا غيث فيها (`quranic:root_001596:B005/m01`); مهواة ومفازة مهلكة (`quranic:root_001596:B006/m01`); إخلاف النوء (`quranic:root_000450:B003/m01`); خواء الجوف (`quranic:root_000450:B005/m01`); قرع وانكشاف وخلو (`quranic:root_001219:B008/m01`); دوام الشيء وبقاؤه (`quranic:root_000142:B001/m01`); البقية وما يبقى من الشيء (`quranic:root_000142:B002/m01`); الإبقاء عفوا واستحياء (`quranic:root_000142:B003/m01`); حبس بعض الشيء وادخاره (`quranic:root_000142:B004/m01`); يبس الشجر (`quranic:root_000217:B008/m01`); ما بالدار نافخ ضرمة (`quranic:root_001529:B005/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ء ف ك`: 69:9 (مُؤْتَفِكَٰتُ); `خ ط ء`: 69:9 (خَاطِئَةِ); `ه ل ك`: 69:5 (أُهْلِكُ), 69:6 (أُهْلِكُ); `خ و ي`: 69:7 (خَاوِيَةٍ); `ق ر ع`: 69:4 (قَارِعَةِ); `ب ق ي`: 69:8 (بَاقِيَةٍ); `ج ب ل`: 69:14 (جِبَالُ); `ن ف خ`: 69:13 (نُفِخَ/نَفْخَةٌ)","synthesis":"A rainless, burned tract and a star-season that yields no water lead to hollow bellies, dead wood, stripped enclosures, an empty house, and a killing waste. Against that extinction, remaining, remnant, mercy, and saved reserve become sharply material counter-images: survival is what has not been stripped out of the place.","ayah_refs":["69:9","69:5","69:6","69:7","69:4","69:8","69:14","69:13"]},{"parent_channel":"P1: A message is sent, heard, and kept","parent_semantic_invariant":"Communication is a custody chain in which a charge is dispatched, carried, received, retained, and made recallable.","parent_surprising_reach":"The communication chain is materialized as herd after herd, flowing provision, a carried burden, an inward vessel, a legal deed, and an ear that acts as a gate of permission.","key":"D","name":"Spoken name, reputation, and public recurrence","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"Repeated speech makes a person or event present in communal memory as name, fame, praise, or blame.","active_motifs":"جريان الذكر على اللسان (`quranic:root_000516:B004/m01`); ذكر المرء شرف وصيت (`quranic:root_000516:B007/m01`); الاسم تنويه ودلالة (`quranic:root_000745:B005/m01`); الصيت الحسن المنتشر (`quranic:root_000745:B008/m01`); رياء الناس (`quranic:root_000531:B005/m01`); الرسول والرسالة (`quranic:root_000563:B002/m01`); استقامة واعتدال واستواء (`quranic:root_001273:B008/m01`); ردع يقرع السامع (`quranic:root_001219:B006/m01`); الجَعْلة اسم مكان (`quranic:root_000248:B011/m01`); العلم بموضع أو يوم (`quranic:root_000450:B008/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ذ ك ر`: 69:12 (تَذْكِرَةً); `س م و`: 69:16 (سَّمَآءُ); `ر ء ي`: 69:7 (تَرَى), 69:8 (تَرَىٰ); `ر س ل`: 69:10 (رَسُولَ); `ق و م`: 69:7 (قَوْمَ); `ق ر ع`: 69:4 (قَارِعَةِ); `ج ع ل`: 69:12 (نَجْعَلَ); `خ و ي`: 69:7 (خَاوِيَةٍ)","synthesis":"Tongued mention and carried message give a name public reach; favorable renown can rise, while place-name and named day stabilize reference. Performance for spectators and rebuke expose distorted social uses of visibility, and straight speech supplies the norm by which praise, blame, and reputation are heard.","ayah_refs":["69:12","69:16","69:7","69:8","69:10","69:4"]},{"parent_channel":"P1: Falsehood redirects signs and surfaces","parent_semantic_invariant":"Misrepresentation works by attributing what is not so, coating reality with a misleading surface, or turning perception away from its proper object.","parent_surprising_reach":"Falsehood reaches bodily motion and materials: milk promises continuity then stops, an animal runs then checks, cloth lies by its colors, metal is plated, and a charm turns a face.","key":"A","name":"False report, attribution, and inward self-deception","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"A speaker or community asserts what is contrary to fact, assigns the lie to another, or houses deception in the self.","active_motifs":"خلاف الصدق (`quranic:root_001290:B001/m01`); نسبة الشيء أو صاحبه إلى الكذب (`quranic:root_001290:B002/m01`); النفس الكذوب (`quranic:root_001290:B008/m01`); الإفك كذب مصروف عن الصدق (`quranic:root_000041:B002/m01`); عقل مأفوك وحيلة مصروفة (`quranic:root_000041:B006/m01`); ردع يقرع السامع (`quranic:root_001219:B006/m01`); رياء الناس (`quranic:root_000531:B005/m01`); الوقيعة في الناس (`quranic:root_001675:B013/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ك ذ ب`: 69:4 (كَذَّبَتْ); `ء ف ك`: 69:9 (مُؤْتَفِكَٰتُ); `ق ر ع`: 69:4 (قَارِعَةِ); `ر ء ي`: 69:7 (تَرَى), 69:8 (تَرَىٰ); `و ق ع`: 69:15 (وَقَعَتِ/وَاقِعَةُ)","synthesis":"Lie, accusation of lying, deceptive self, turned judgment, and backbiting form a single testimonial failure. Public performance seeks witnesses rather than truth, while rebuke presses the hearer to reverse direction and re-enter truthful judgment.","ayah_refs":["69:4","69:9","69:7","69:8","69:15"]},{"parent_channel":"P1: Falsehood redirects signs and surfaces","parent_semantic_invariant":"Misrepresentation works by attributing what is not so, coating reality with a misleading surface, or turning perception away from its proper object.","parent_surprising_reach":"Falsehood reaches bodily motion and materials: milk promises continuity then stops, an animal runs then checks, cloth lies by its colors, metal is plated, and a charm turns a face.","key":"B","name":"Painted cloth, plated metal, and turning charm","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"A fabricated outer appearance induces an observer or target to take one thing for another.","active_motifs":"الكذابة ثوب يكذب بحاله (`quranic:root_001290:B009/m01`); كسوة المعدن بماء الذهب أو الفضة (`quranic:root_001458:B005/m01`); خرزة تقبل وجها إلى وجه (`quranic:root_001198:B016/m01`); رقية تمسك وتحبس (`quranic:root_000018:B004/m01`); مرأى ومنظر ومرآة (`quranic:root_000531:B006/m01`); رئي من الجن (`quranic:root_000531:B008/m01`); الصورة والهيئة المصورة (`quranic:root_000891:B003/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ك ذ ب`: 69:4 (كَذَّبَتْ); `م و ه`: 69:11 (مَآءُ); `ق ب ل`: 69:9 (قَبْلَ); `ء خ ذ`: 69:10 (أَخَذَ/أَخْذَةً); `ر ء ي`: 69:7 (تَرَى), 69:8 (تَرَىٰ); `ص و ر`: 69:13 (صُّورِ)","synthesis":"Multicolored cloth 'lies' by its state, plating presents base metal as precious, and a bead or binding charm turns attention and action. Mirror-image and a presenting spirit supply receivers for that manufactured appearance; formed shape can be mentally real while materially misleading.","ayah_refs":["69:4","69:11","69:9","69:10","69:7","69:8","69:13"]},{"parent_channel":"P1: Falsehood redirects signs and surfaces","parent_semantic_invariant":"Misrepresentation works by attributing what is not so, coating reality with a misleading surface, or turning perception away from its proper object.","parent_surprising_reach":"Falsehood reaches bodily motion and materials: milk promises continuity then stops, an animal runs then checks, cloth lies by its colors, metal is plated, and a charm turns a face.","key":"D","name":"Promised continuation that abruptly fails","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"An action appears likely to persist but stops, delays, or proves unable to complete its course.","active_motifs":"ما كذب أن فعل أي ما لبث (`quranic:root_001290:B005/m01`); كذب لبن الناقة إذا ذهب ولم يدم (`quranic:root_001290:B006/m01`); كذب الوحشي إذا جرى ثم وقف (`quranic:root_001290:B007/m01`); سكوت لا يبدئ ولا يعيد (`quranic:root_001058:B003/m01`); ضعف وقصور عن الفعل (`quranic:root_000985:B001/m01`); فوت وسبق وإفلات من الطلب (`quranic:root_000985:B002/m01`); جمود ووقوف وكلال (`quranic:root_001273:B016/m01`); ترقب الشيء وانتظاره بالبصر (`quranic:root_000142:B005/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ك ذ ب`: 69:4 (كَذَّبَتْ); `ع و د`: 69:4 (عَادٌۢ), 69:6 (عَادٌ); `ع ج ز`: 69:7 (أَعْجَازُ); `ق و م`: 69:7 (قَوْمَ); `ب ق ي`: 69:8 (بَاقِيَةٍ)","synthesis":"Immediate action can belie delay, milk can belie expected duration, and a running animal can halt to look back. Speechlessness, incapacity, escape, and a stalled mount turn failed continuation into a bodily scene; watchful waiting measures the gap between expectation and arrival.","ayah_refs":["69:4","69:6","69:7","69:8"]},{"parent_channel":"P1: Sovereignty rests on raised supports","parent_semantic_invariant":"Rule is sustained by a hierarchy of throne, upper position, structural mainstay, delegated care, and communal covenant.","parent_surprising_reach":"Royal elevation is tested through architecture and husbandry: a throne may be broken, a roof propped, a well timbered, a flock led, and a polity maintained by water and covenant.","key":"A","name":"King, throne, and superior position","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"A sovereign occupies a raised seat and exercises rule through rank, power, and recognized leadership.","active_motifs":"ربوبية وملك وسيادة (`quranic:root_000532:B001/m01`); علم رباني (`quranic:root_000532:B003/m01`); رباني الملاحين (`quranic:root_000532:B017/m01`); المِلْك والتصرف (`quranic:root_001444:B002/m01`); المُلك والسلطان (`quranic:root_001444:B003/m01`); مِلاك الأمر وعِماده (`quranic:root_001444:B005/m01`); المتقدم القائد في الحيوان (`quranic:root_001444:B008/m01`); السرير المرتفع للملك (`quranic:root_001000:B001/m01`); قوام الأمر والعز إذا ثبت أو ثل (`quranic:root_001000:B006/m01`); علو المنزلة والفضل (`quranic:root_001188:B002/m01`); علو القهر والغلبة (`quranic:root_001188:B003/m01`); رعاية وحفظ وولاية (`quranic:root_001273:B004/m01`); قوام وعماد ومعاش (`quranic:root_001273:B009/m01`); سادات كالجِبال (`quranic:root_000217:B012/m01`); مقروع مختار وخيار (`quranic:root_001219:B007/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ر ب ب`: 69:10 (رَبِّ), 69:17 (رَبِّ); `م ل ك`: 69:17 (مَلَكُ); `ع ر ش`: 69:17 (عَرْشَ); `ف و ق`: 69:17 (فَوْقَ); `ق و م`: 69:7 (قَوْمَ); `ج ب ل`: 69:14 (جِبَالُ); `ق ر ع`: 69:4 (قَارِعَةِ)","synthesis":"Lordship, learned divine formation, captaincy, ownership, kingship, and the affair's mainstay converge on the raised throne. Superior rank and coercive power explain the vertical relation, while recognized leaders and chosen chiefs translate it into the governed community; a 'broken throne' names the collapse of the whole arrangement.","ayah_refs":["69:10","69:17","69:7","69:14","69:4"]},{"parent_channel":"P1: Herd life follows generation, movement, and equipment","parent_semantic_invariant":"Animal life is organized through mating, gestation, maturation, herd circulation, milking intervals, shelter, and predation.","parent_surprising_reach":"The lexical network turns abstract completion and return into a full pastoral economy, from conception and birth through age, watering, tack, shelter, and the hunter's blind.","key":"A","name":"Mating, conception, and approaching birth","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"Male and female animals meet, conception becomes visible, and a carried young approaches emergence.","active_motifs":"ناقة بلغت حق الحمل والانتفاع (`quranic:root_000347:B008/m01`); تمام حال الحيوان وقوته (`quranic:root_000347:B013/m01`); أحق من الخيل يطابق خطوه أو يشتد بدنه (`quranic:root_000347:B014/m01`); ضراب الفحل وإنزاؤه (`quranic:root_001219:B003/m01`); ظهور حمل الناقة أو الشاة (`quranic:root_000531:B010/m01`); تحصن الفرس وصيرورته فحلا (`quranic:root_000609:B018/m01`); الحَمْل الباطن والثمر (`quranic:root_000357:B002/m01`); الحَمَل من الضأن (`quranic:root_000357:B009/m01`); ماء الفحل في الرحم (`quranic:root_001458:B004/m01`); اشتهاء الأنثى للفحل (`quranic:root_000248:B009/m01`); فرخ النعام (`quranic:root_000248:B010/m01`); الحامل تدنو ولادتها (`quranic:root_000548:B005/m01`); رفع الحمار رأسه عند الحمل (`quranic:root_001000:B009/m01`); تغذية ونشوء (`quranic:root_000537:B005/m01`); انتفاخ الشيء وامتلاؤه بالريح (`quranic:root_001529:B002/m01`); الطغيا الصغير من بقر الوحش (`quranic:root_000936:B009/m01`); تطاول الفحل على الشول (`quranic:root_000745:B003/m01`); إلقاء الثقل في المخالطة (`quranic:root_000482:B009/m01`); المواقعة في الجماع (`quranic:root_001675:B014/m01`); ترية الحيض (`quranic:root_000531:B007/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ح ق ق`: 69:1 (حَآقَّةُ), 69:2 (حَآقَّةُ), 69:3 (حَآقَّةُ); `ق ر ع`: 69:4 (قَارِعَةِ); `ر ء ي`: 69:7 (تَرَى), 69:8 (تَرَىٰ); `ر و ح`: 69:6 (رِيحٍ); `ح م ل`: 69:11 (حَمَلْ), 69:14 (حُمِلَتِ), 69:17 (يَحْمِلُ); `م و ه`: 69:11 (مَآءُ); `ج ع ل`: 69:12 (نَجْعَلَ); `ر ج و`: 69:17 (أَرْجَآئِ); `ع ر ش`: 69:17 (عَرْشَ); `ر ب و`: 69:10 (رَّابِيَةً); `ن ف خ`: 69:13 (نُفِخَ/نَفْخَةٌ); `ط غ و`: unavailable (surface root missing); `س م و`: 69:16 (سَّمَآءُ); `د ك ك`: 69:14 (دُكَّ/دَكَّةً); `و ق ع`: 69:15 (وَقَعَتِ/وَاقِعَةُ)","synthesis":"Breeding readiness, mounting, deposited seed, visible pregnancy, inward burden, swelling, menstrual sign, and the nearness of birth form a continuous generational scene. A stallion rising over mares and bodily weight in intercourse make coupling explicit; young camel, lamb, ostrich chick, and wild-cattle calf mark successive products. The `ط غ و` calf motif has no available surface-root anchor.","ayah_refs":["69:1","69:2","69:3","69:4","69:7","69:8","69:6","69:11","69:14","69:17","69:12","69:10","69:13","69:16","69:15"]},{"parent_channel":"P1: Herd life follows generation, movement, and equipment","parent_semantic_invariant":"Animal life is organized through mating, gestation, maturation, herd circulation, milking intervals, shelter, and predation.","parent_surprising_reach":"The lexical network turns abstract completion and return into a full pastoral economy, from conception and birth through age, watering, tack, shelter, and the hunter's blind.","key":"B","name":"Age, bodily form, and usable maturity","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"An animal passes from youth into a bodily state suited to work, breeding, or valuation.","active_motifs":"عود مسن فيه بقايا قوة (`quranic:root_001058:B008/m01`); كبر وقدم حتى يسمى عجوزا (`quranic:root_000985:B003/m01`); آخر الولد عند كبر الأبوين (`quranic:root_000985:B007/m01`); العُتِيّ من الكبر (`quranic:root_000981:B002/m01`); غلظ الخلقة والجسم (`quranic:root_000217:B003/m01`); خلقة مطبوعة (`quranic:root_000217:B004/m01`); قامة وقوام الجسم والطول (`quranic:root_001273:B011/m01`); السعة والانبساط الحسي (`quranic:root_000609:B009/m01`); ناقة بلغت حق الحمل والانتفاع (`quranic:root_000347:B008/m01`); تمام حال الحيوان وقوته (`quranic:root_000347:B013/m01`); أحق من الخيل يطابق خطوه أو يشتد بدنه (`quranic:root_000347:B014/m01`); صرصور البختي (`quranic:root_000857:B003/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ع و د`: 69:4 (عَادٌۢ), 69:6 (عَادٌ); `ع ج ز`: 69:7 (أَعْجَازُ); `ع ت و`: 69:6 (عَاتِيَةٍ); `ج ب ل`: 69:14 (جِبَالُ); `ق و م`: 69:7 (قَوْمَ); `ر و ح`: 69:6 (رِيحٍ); `ح ق ق`: 69:1 (حَآقَّةُ), 69:2 (حَآقَّةُ), 69:3 (حَآقَّةُ); `ص ر ص ر`: 69:6 (صَرْصَرٍ)","synthesis":"Old camel, late child, extreme age, thick bodily formation, inherited disposition, stature, breadth of foot, Bactrian camel, completed fatness, and matched stride describe maturity as a change in usable form. The same completion may qualify an animal for load, breeding, or price.","ayah_refs":["69:4","69:6","69:7","69:14","69:1","69:2","69:3"]},{"parent_channel":"P1: Herd life follows generation, movement, and equipment","parent_semantic_invariant":"Animal life is organized through mating, gestation, maturation, herd circulation, milking intervals, shelter, and predation.","parent_surprising_reach":"The lexical network turns abstract completion and return into a full pastoral economy, from conception and birth through age, watering, tack, shelter, and the hunter's blind.","key":"C","name":"Herd return, alternating groups, and milking rhythm","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"Animals move out and back in ordered groups, pause at water, and yield milk on recurring intervals.","active_motifs":"رجوع بعد انصراف وتثنية بعد بدء (`quranic:root_001058:B001/m01`); عيادة ومعادة وزيارة راجعة (`quranic:root_001058:B005/m01`); الرواح والعشي ورد الماشية (`quranic:root_000609:B005/m01`); المراوحة والتناوب (`quranic:root_000609:B008/m01`); طرفا النهار المتعاقبان (`quranic:root_000859:B005/m01`); إبل تختلف ذهابا ومجيئا (`quranic:root_000859:B006/m01`); انتصاب وقيام بالبدن (`quranic:root_001273:B002/m01`); جمود ووقوف وكلال (`quranic:root_001273:B016/m01`); انتصاف النهار وقائم الظهيرة (`quranic:root_001273:B017/m01`); قوام في قوائم الشاة (`quranic:root_001273:B020/m01`); رجوع اللبن بين الحلبتين (`quranic:root_001188:B006/m01`); أخذ الشيء جرعات أو دفعات (`quranic:root_001188:B007/m01`); السير السهل واللين (`quranic:root_000563:B003/m01`); التتابع والقطع (`quranic:root_000563:B005/m01`); اللبن والدر المتتابع (`quranic:root_000563:B006/m01`); أداة الحمل ومركوبه (`quranic:root_000357:B006/m01`); العدد سبعة وما يتفرع منه (`quranic:root_000669:B001/m01`); تكسّر التهالك في المشي والغنج (`quranic:root_001596:B003/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ع و د`: 69:4 (عَادٌۢ), 69:6 (عَادٌ); `ر و ح`: 69:6 (رِيحٍ); `ص ر ع`: 69:7 (صَرْعَىٰ); `ق و م`: 69:7 (قَوْمَ); `ف و ق`: 69:17 (فَوْقَ); `ر س ل`: 69:10 (رَسُولَ); `ح م ل`: 69:11 (حَمَلْ), 69:14 (حُمِلَتِ), 69:17 (يَحْمِلُ); `س ب ع`: 69:7 (سَبْعَ); `ه ل ك`: 69:5 (أُهْلِكُ), 69:6 (أُهْلِكُ)","synthesis":"Return and repeated visitation become pastoral circulation: morning and evening groups cross, one herd arrives as another leaves, animals stand or tire, and the day reaches its central station. Milk returns between milkings and is taken in intervals; easy or swaying gait, successive droves, abundant yield, transport stock, and numbered watering cycles maintain the rhythm.","ayah_refs":["69:4","69:6","69:7","69:17","69:10","69:11","69:14","69:5"]},{"parent_channel":"P1: Plants are raised, selected, and made valuable","parent_semantic_invariant":"Cultivation converts water and soil into growth, then separates, selects, stores, and values the product.","parent_surprising_reach":"Palm trunks open onto seedbed, cloud, sap, sieve, jewelry, market, and reserved running strength rather than remaining a single botanical emblem.","key":"C","name":"Sifting, choosing, reserving, and pricing","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"A harvest or stock is separated so that the best portion can be saved, displayed, and assigned value.","active_motifs":"تصفية الدقيق بالمنخل (`quranic:root_001483:B002/m01`); اختيار الأفضل (`quranic:root_001483:B003/m01`); تنخيل الثلج والودق (`quranic:root_001483:B004/m01`); مقروع مختار وخيار (`quranic:root_001219:B007/m01`); العوض والقيمة في البيع (`quranic:root_000206:B001/m01`); قيمة وتقويم وتسعير (`quranic:root_001273:B010/m01`); نفاق السوق (`quranic:root_001273:B018/m01`); حبس بعض الشيء وادخاره (`quranic:root_000142:B004/m01`); تمام حال الحيوان وقوته (`quranic:root_000347:B013/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ن خ ل`: 69:7 (نَخْلٍ); `ق ر ع`: 69:4 (قَارِعَةِ); `ث م ن`: 69:7 (ثَمَٰنِيَةَ), 69:17 (ثَمَٰنِيَةٌ); `ق و م`: 69:7 (قَوْمَ); `ب ق ي`: 69:8 (بَاقِيَةٍ); `ح ق ق`: 69:1 (حَآقَّةُ), 69:2 (حَآقَّةُ), 69:3 (حَآقَّةُ)","synthesis":"Sieving separates flour and bran; selection extracts the best; falling rain or snow is imagined through the same perforated motion. Choice stock, price, appraisal, active market, reserved running power, and finished fatness continue the operation from processing into exchange.","ayah_refs":["69:7","69:4","69:17","69:8","69:1","69:2","69:3"]},{"parent_channel":"P1: Plants are raised, selected, and made valuable","parent_semantic_invariant":"Cultivation converts water and soil into growth, then separates, selects, stores, and values the product.","parent_surprising_reach":"Palm trunks open onto seedbed, cloud, sap, sieve, jewelry, market, and reserved running strength rather than remaining a single botanical emblem.","key":"D","name":"Garden produce, insects, and damaged wood","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"A cultivated plot contains edible growth alongside small creatures that inhabit or damage it.","active_motifs":"قرع اليقطين (`quranic:root_001219:B009/m01`); دويبة الجعلان (`quranic:root_000248:B008/m01`); دويبة الصرصر (`quranic:root_000857:B001/m01`); الأَرَضَة آكلة الخشب (`quranic:root_000025:B010/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ق ر ع`: 69:4 (قَارِعَةِ); `ج ع ل`: 69:12 (نَجْعَلَ); `ص ر ص ر`: 69:6 (صَرْصَرٍ); `ء ر ض`: 69:14 (أَرْضُ)","synthesis":"Gourd, dung beetle, small chirping creature, and wood-eating termite create a compact garden ecology. ## Your response

Write in tr. Produce your response as three parts, in this order. If you return one combined response, label the parts clearly. If an orchestrator asks you to write the parts into separate files, omit wrapper labels from the prose and evidence files; the file path already supplies the label.

1. **The prose** — per the output contract in `COMMENTARY_SPEC.md` §5 and the task document above: continuous, single voice, no provenance markers, no headers named after evidence layers.
2. **The evidence surface** — separate from the prose, addressable per phrase, per `PRINCIPLES.md` §12.
3. A section headed exactly `=== PROMPT FRICTION ===` reporting honestly where the specification above was unclear, self-contradictory, underspecified, or impossible to follow, and any point where you had to invent a rule to proceed. Report this section every time, even if the run went cleanly — say so explicitly if you found nothing. This is how the specification gets improved.

## V2 Rendering Profile — 5.6 Sol High

This run tests whether `5.6-sol-high` can increase lexical depth while preserving
reader-facing clarity.

Increase depth through semantic precision, not additional bulk.

Before drafting, silently build a coverage ledger:

- include every `must_integrate` topic;
- include every `candidate` with a reader payoff not already expressed;
- if `v12_reader_responses` is absent because the default workflow retired
  per-ayah focus runs, do not infer a `stage_00` isolated response or
  confidence movement;
- if `v12_focus_trace_hermetic` is present, use its `baseline_models`,
  `context_deltas`, and `surprising_valid_outliers` as reconstructed
  before/after evidence. Do not call it a staged reveal transcript. Preserve
  surprising outliers when they remain anchored in this ayah, especially
  secondary split-root activations;
- include a regular reader-walk, plus/minus-5 reader-walk, whole-surah reading,
  or cross-run-publication item only when it adds a distinct retrospective
  insight or coverage check;
- use branch inventories to support explanations; they create no standalone
  prose obligation. When fallback inventories are present, treat them as
  restricted to this ayah's roots and anchored citations, not as a whole-surah
  obligation.
- treat `root_lexicon` as full dictionary/gloss support, not branch-filtered.
  It uses QAC-to-Furuq root mapping; when a QAC root is split, non-dominant
  Furuq targets are included as additional root entries and recorded in
  coverage. If multiple QAC roots map to the same Furuq root, the shared entry
  records all source roots in `qac_roots_ar` / `qac_root_mappings`; use those
  fields for evidence attribution when present, not the legacy single `root_ar`
  alone. Dictionary/gloss branches are evidence support, not independent
  obligations, unless tied to this ayah's word, topic, reader payoff, or cited
  relation. Full field means all distinct reader payoffs from activated
  material, not every dictionary branch for the root.
- if `channel_generated_outputs` lists quran-data files and this run gives you
  file access, read only those listed files when channel-family/path detail is
  necessary. Treat them as candidate/family/path evidence, not as an adjudicated
  channel ledger. State B channel restrictions still apply.

For each critical word, preserve these distinct layers when available:

1. local grammatical work;
2. locally selected sense;
3. coherent pressure supplied by activated or cited root branches;
4. one form, sound, rarity, or variant observation with a unique payoff;
5. later contextual change, compressed to its final reader-visible result.

Do not flatten these layers into a general metaphor. Deepen generic summaries by
naming the exact lexical mechanism. Ordering grammar and local meaning first is
grounding, not truth-ranking.

At first mention of an ayah word, use a structured Arabic surface span:
`{ar:surface_form, tr:Turkish-readable transliteration, gloss:target-language meaning}`.
Use the same full span again when the prose returns to that word after moving to
another word or another paragraph. Inside one short local sequence, after a full
span has just been given, a Turkish label or transliteration is enough. Explain
every technical term in the same sentence. Keep raw roots, root skeletons,
letter-by-letter root transliterations, and branch IDs in the evidence surface.
In prose, attach root discussion to the surface word:
`{ar:ٱلْعَادِيَاتِ, tr:el-âdiyât, gloss:koşup atılanlar} kelimesinin bağlı
olduğu kök alanı...`, not `ʿ-d-v kökü...`.

A paragraph may begin with the ayah's surface, a concrete image, or a
reader-facing claim. It may not begin with a bare grammar label or stacked
abstractions. Prefer "Âyet önce hamdi Allah'a verir; sabit ad cümlesi bu hamdi
yerleşik bir hüküm olarak taşır" over "Bu âyet, tek bir isim cümlesiyle yerleşik
bir hüküm kurar."

For this short ayah, keep at least two-thirds of the prose on its own wording.
Compress all later developments into at most three paragraphs and end with one
plain synthesis paragraph.

Use `v12_cross_run_publication`, if present, only as a compact coverage/priority
check derived from regular and plus/minus-5 reader runs. Do not copy it as prose,
and do not let it override local bundle evidence.

Use an explicit negative predicate only to correct a likely misconception,
protect the primary sense from replacement, or preserve live counter-evidence.
Default ceiling for this ayah: three. Never stack two negatives in one sentence.
During final revision, rewrite all other negatives as positive predication.
Do not drive negation to zero by default: if no explicit negative is needed,
include a profile-specific style audit in friction confirming that there was no
live misconception, replacement risk, or counter-evidence requiring one.

Pass only if every paragraph has one distinct reader payoff and the prose remains
clear with the evidence surface closed.
